2026-10-20T06:18:25.9981753Z env:
2026-10-20T06:18:25.9982104Z   UV_PYTHON_INSTALL_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/uv-python-dir

2026-10-20T06:18:25.9990205Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve main with a doubled Prefill batch: 8192 tokens per DP rank (chunked_prefill_size 32768)

Why: the Prefill MegaMoE kernel work has reached its structural limits for
now. The FC1 K loop runs at about DeepGEMM's standalone efficiency, the FC2
scatter is NVLink-bound, and the header-only 2-warpgroup restructure
(39374402355) did not shorten the K loop. Of the 1550 us per call at 4096
tokens per rank, about 180 us are per-call fixed costs (dispatch and its
NVLink barrier about 40 us; the grid sync, NVLink barrier and combine tail
about 140 us). Every forward also pays per-forward costs: launches, the
EAGLE3 draft extend, sampling and scheduling. Today each Prefill DP rank
runs one 3994-token request per forward (request-aligned chunks, 4096 tokens
per rank). Putting two requests in one forward (8064 tokens after page
rounding, below 8192) spreads those fixed costs over twice the tokens.
TTFT has room: on main (39225913979) the average is 1659 ms and p90 1825 ms
against the 3 s SLA. A forward of about twice the length adds roughly
0.3-0.5 s.

What: configs/firmus-framework.json prefill.chunked_prefill_size 16384 ->
32768 (8192 per DP rank over DP4). Since f0fed01 sglang.sbatch passes this
value to the Prefill workers and sizes their MegaMoE symmetric buffer to the
per-rank batch (SGLANG_OPT_DEEPGEMM_MEGA_MOE_NUM_MAX_TOKENS_PER_RANK 8192;
Decode stays 4096). Nothing else changes. The image is 317ab7c7, and the
SGLang and DeepGEMM overlays are main's. Precision is unchanged; only the
batch composition changes.

Expected: Prefill throughput +3-5% from the amortized fixed costs (MegaMoE
alone is about -6% per token), with higher TTFT. Risks: more TTFT failures
in the start-up burst, and Prefill memory (the MegaMoE buffer doubles, plus
activations for 8192 rows).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
